Kahan tootti hai: Graph ink colour ko chromatographic pigment colour na samjhein.
p007-i7 From Figure 11.3a can you determine the wavelength (colour of light) at which chlorophyll a shows the maximum absorption? Does it show another absorption peak at any other wavelengths too? If yes, which one?
p007-i11 Figure 11.3a: three absorption curves against wavelength. The cyan chlorophyll a curve has a major blue-region peak near 420–430 nm and a red-region peak near 660–670 nm, with low absorption across much of the intervening region. The green chlorophyll b curve has a tall peak near 460 nm and a smaller red-region peak near 645 nm. The yellow carotenoid curve absorbs mainly in the blue region, with broad peaks around 450–480 nm, and falls to near baseline beyond about 520 nm. The vertical axis reads 'Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments'. The wavelength scale is shared with the lower panels.
figure11.4-k8
Ab Figure 11.3b mein same horizontal wavelength positions par actual photosynthetic output dekho. Vertical label Rate of photosynthesis, measured by O₂ release, hai; rate ka matlab fixed time mein hua photosynthesis hai. Absorption spectrum pigment ka light capture dikhata hai, jabki action spectrum wavelength ke saath photosynthesis ki effectiveness dikhata hai. Black curve blue region mein high hai, green se yellow-green region near 550–570 nm mein broad minimum par aati hai. Phir red region near 680 nm mein high hoti hai aur 700 nm ke paas sharply girti hai. Kya green region mein photosynthesis bilkul zero hai? Dekho, curve intermediate wavelengths par bhi baseline se upar hai, isliye activity low hai, absent nahi. Chlorophyll a ke high absorption aur high photosynthetic rate dono blue aur red regions mein milte hain.
Screen par: Panel a quiet rahe; aligned panel b mein O₂-release-based action curve blue peak se green minimum aur red peak tak trace ho; vertical rate label highlight.

p007-i17 Figure 11.3b: black action-spectrum curve shows photosynthetic rate measured by O₂ release against wavelength. The curve is high in the blue region, falls toward a broad minimum in the green to yellow-green region around 550–570 nm, rises again to a red-region maximum around 680 nm, and drops steeply near 700 nm. It is not zero across the intermediate visible region. Horizontal positions align with the shared wavelength scale below.

Aksar log sochte hain ki chief pigment hone ka matlab sirf chlorophyll a hi kaam karta hai, par asal mein doosre pigments bhi light capture mein contribute karte hain. Dekho, main role hona doosre pigments ke contribution ko cancel nahi karta.
Screen par: Blue/red matching spectral regions se chlorophyll a—chief pigment tak connection; baaki named pigments paas mein present rahen.

p007-i9 Hence, we can conclude that chlorophyll a is the chief pigment associated with photosynthesis.

ruko, socho11.4-k11
Socho, phir aage badhenge: kya chlorophyll a absorption aur photosynthesis action spectrum ka complete one-to-one overlap milta hai? Agar chlorophyll a ki absorption hi poora response samjha deti, toh intermediate region mein extra action response kyun dikhta? Dekho, curves completely overlap nahi karti, aur yeh mismatch batata hai ki chlorophyll a ke apne absorption ke alawa bhi light capture ka contribution hai. Is conclusion se chief pigment ki importance kam nahi hoti; ab uski madad karne wale pigments ka role khulega.
Screen par: Panel c ki intermediate wavelength band mein dono curves ke unmatched patterns; blue/red correspondence quietly visible rahe.
Jawab: Complete overlap nahi hai; other pigments ke light capture se bhi photosynthetic activity support hoti hai.
Aam galti: Incomplete overlap chlorophyll a ko unimportant nahi banata, aur green-region activity ko zero bhi nahi batata.
p007-i10 But by looking at Figure 11.3c can you say that there is a complete one-to-one overlap between the absorption spectrum of chlorophyll a and the action spectrum of photosynthesis?
samjhana11.4-k12
Ab mismatch ka reason jodo: thylakoid membranes mein chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur doosre carotenoids accessory pigments ki tarah kaam karte hain. Accessory pigments main pigment ko support karne wale light-absorbing pigments hain. Yeh different wavelengths absorb karke captured energy chlorophyll a tak transfer karte hain, isliye usable wavelength range badhti hai. Yahaan pigment se pigment ko energy transfer samjho, electron ka wahi raasta nahi. Blue aur red mein activity generally high hai, lekin doosri visible wavelengths bhi photosynthesis support kar sakti hain. In pigments ka doosra fayda kya hai? Dekho, yeh chlorophyll a ko photo-oxidation, yaani light-associated damaging oxidation, se protect bhi karte hain. Isliye accessory pigments light capture ko broaden karne ke saath light-related damage se protection bhi dete hain.
Screen par: Realistic thylakoid membrane close-up mein named accessory pigments se chlorophyll a tak energy arrows; phir chlorophyll a ke paas protection symbol add ho.
Terms: accessory pigments = Chlorophyll a ko light capture aur protection mein support karne wale pigments.; photo-oxidation = Light se associated oxidation jo pigment ko damage kar sakta hai.
Yaad: 11.3-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i2
p008-i1 These graphs, together, show that most of the photosynthesis takes place in the blue and red regions of the spectrum; some photosynthesis does take place at the other wavelengths of the visible spectrum. Let us see how this happens. Though chlorophyll is the major pigment responsible for trapping light, other thylakoid pigments like chlorophyll b, xanthophylls and carotenoids, which are called accessory pigments, also absorb light and transfer the energy to chlorophyll a. Indeed, they not only enable a wider range of wavelength of incoming light to be utilised for photosynthesis but also protect chlorophyll a from photo-oxidation.
asli duniya11.4-k13
Prolonged darkness mein green leaf kabhi yellow ya pale green hone lagti hai. Yellow pigment kya usi waqt naya aaya hai? Dekho, yellow pigments pehle se present ho sakte hain, lekin dominant chlorophyll unka colour mask karta hai. Lambe darkness period mein chlorophyll ka replenishment impair hota hai aur uski amount ghat sakti hai. Relatively persistent carotenoids, including yellow xanthophylls, tab visible ho jaate hain; kuch chlorophyll bachne par leaf pale green lag sakti hai. Is situation mein yellow pigments chlorophyll se relatively zyada stable dikhte hain, lekin iska matlab har pigment har condition mein equally persistent hona nahi hai.
Screen par: Same leaf ka green se pale-green/yellow comparison; pigment bars mein chlorophyll ghate, pehle se present yellow pigment contribution reveal ho.
Kitaab se bahar: Prolonged darkness mein impaired chlorophyll replenishment, pigment masking aur yellow pigments ki relative persistence exercise foundation ke liye added hain.

asli duniya11.4-k14
Healthy shade leaf ki situation prolonged darkness se alag hai: shade mein light kam milti hai, bilkul gayab nahi hoti. Same plant ki shaded leaves aksar sun-exposed leaves se darker green dikh sakti hain. Kam light mein darker green kyun faydemand ho sakta hai? Dekho, leaf available light capture karne ke liye pigment investment badal sakti hai, commonly tissue mass ke relative chlorophyll aur chlorophyll b ka proportion badhta hai. Is adjustment se chlorophyll a:b ratio, yaani a ki amount ka b se comparison, kam ho sakta hai. Pigment composition ka yeh adjustment shade acclimation hai; darker colour ka matlab automatically higher photosynthetic rate nahi hai.
Screen par: Same plant ki healthy shaded aur sun-exposed leaves; shaded leaf ke paas fewer incoming light rays aur relative chlorophyll b investment ka larger bar.
Terms: shade acclimation = Kam light ke according plant ki structure aur functioning ka adjustment.; chlorophyll a:b ratio = Chlorophyll a ki amount ka chlorophyll b ki amount se comparison.

→ Ab pigments ki light capture samajh aa gayi; light reaction mein dekhenge ki unka organised arrangement captured energy ko ATP aur NADPH banane ke kaam se kaise jodta hai.
11.5 WHAT IS LIGHT REACTION? · 9 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 7 min
Maqsad: Ab pigments ki captured energy ko follow karke samjhenge ki light reaction ki organised machinery chemical kaam kaise shuru karti hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.5-k1
Ab pigments ki captured energy ko follow karke samjhenge ki light reaction ki organised machinery chemical kaam kaise shuru karti hai. Photochemical phase wahi directly light-driven reaction set hai, jise hum membrane system par locate kar chuke hain. Is phase mein chaar main events jodo: light absorption, water splitting, oxygen release, aur ATP tatha NADPH formation. Water splitting aur oxygen release connected events hain; inka detailed mechanism aage kholenge. ATP aur NADPH kyun banane hain? Dekho, ATP usable energy aur NADPH reducing power supply karta hai, isliye captured light baad ki sugar synthesis ke kaam aa sakti hai.
Screen par: Realistic chloroplast cutaway mein thylakoid membrane highlighted; paas chaar simple event tiles: light absorption, water splitting, O₂ release, ATP + NADPH formation.
Yaad: 11.3-k8, 11.3-k6
p008-i3 Light reactions or the ‘Photochemical’ phase include light absorption, water splitting, oxygen release, and the formation of high-energy chemical intermediates, ATP and NADPH.
samjhana11.5-k2
Yeh events several protein complexes ki madad se chalte hain; complex mein molecules ek organised working group banate hain. Photosystem light collect karne aur photochemical event shuru karne wali pigment–protein unit hai. Do distinct units hain: Photosystem I, ya PS I, aur Photosystem II, ya PS II. Inmein light harvesting complexes pigments aur proteins ki light-collecting teams hain. Kya PS I ka naam functioning mein pehle aane ka proof hai? Aksar log sochte hain ki PS I pehle function karta hai, par asal mein I aur II discovery ka order batate hain, functioning ka nahi. Actual electron route ko hum agle heading mein dekhenge.
Screen par: Usi thylakoid membrane ka molecular zoom: do separate pigment–protein units PS I aur PS II, dono mein light harvesting complex region highlighted; discovery-order tag dono names se linked.
Terms: Photosystem = Light collect karke photochemical electron transfer shuru karne wali organised pigment–protein unit.; light harvesting complexes (LHC) = Photosystems mein light collect karne wale protein-bound pigments ke groups.
Kitaab se bahar: Protein complex ko organised molecular working group ke roop mein briefly clarify kiya.
p008-i4 Several protein complexes are involved in the process. The pigments are organised into two discrete photochemical light harvesting complexes (LHC) within the Photosystem I (PS I) and Photosystem II (PS II). These are named in the sequence of their discovery, and not in the sequence in which they function during the light reaction.
samjhana11.5-k3
Ek light harvesting complex mein hundreds of pigment molecules proteins se bound hote hain. Photosystem ke collecting pigments milkar antennae banate hain, yaani light-harvesting pigment network. Is collecting network se special reaction-centre chlorophyll a ko alag pehchaano; reaction centre woh site hai jahaan excitation electron transfer shuru karati hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki chlorophyll a sirf reaction centre mein hota hai, par asal mein antenna mein bhi chlorophyll a ke saath doosre pigments hote hain. Itne pigments saath hone se efficiency kyun badhti hai? Dekho, different pigments different wavelengths capture karte hain, aur pehle samjha accessory-pigment energy transfer us collection ko reaction centre tak pahunchata hai.
Screen par: Ek photosystem ka wahi molecular view: protein scaffold par many antenna pigments; unse alag reaction-centre chlorophyll a site; selected coloured light beams collecting pigments tak.
Terms: antennae = Photosystem ka protein-bound pigment network jo light collect karke excitation energy reaction centre tak pahunchata hai.; reaction centre = Photosystem ki special chlorophyll a site jahaan excitation se electron donation shuru hota hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Reaction centre ka functional meaning figure se pehle khola; antenna mein bhi chlorophyll a ki presence clarify ki.
Yaad: 11.4-k12
Exercise ke liye: p022-i2
p008-i5 The LHC are made up of hundreds of pigment molecules bound to proteins. Each photosystem has all the pigments (except one molecule of chlorophyll a) forming a light harvesting system also called antennae (Figure 11.4). These pigments help to make photosynthesis more efficient by absorbing different wavelengths of light.
analogy11.5-k4
Socho, chhat par ek badi collector dish weak incoming signal ko apni broad surface se collect karti hai. Dish ka arrangement signal ko ek focal detector tak concentrate karta hai, jahaan useful electrical response shuru hota hai. Badi collecting surface aur chhota detector alag jobs kyun kar rahe hain? Dekho, broad collector signal pakadne ka chance badhata hai, jabki detector collected signal ko agle useful event mein badalta hai. Yahaan dish antenna pigments ka network hai, focal detector reaction centre hai, aur signal ka focus hona excitation energy ka reaction centre tak pahunchna hai.
Screen par: Collector dish se focal detector tak converging signal paths; saath photosystem ke antenna se reaction centre tak matching energy paths.
Kitaab se bahar: Collector dish aur focal detector ka everyday analogy add kiya.
Kya kis jaisa: Dish antenna pigment network ko, focal detector reaction centre ko, aur converging signal excitation-energy transfer ko represent karta hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Pigments actual dish ki tarah light reflect karke focus nahi karte; absorbed excitation energy molecular interactions se transfer hoti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
figure11.5-k5
Figure 11.4, light harvesting complex, mein Photon label se shuru karo: photon light energy ka ek packet hai, jo peripheral pigment par aata hai. Pigment molecules label collecting cluster ko identify karta hai. Red zigzag route absorbed excitation energy ko pigments ke through Reaction centre tak jaate dikhata hai. Excitation energy ka matlab light absorption se mili extra energy hai. Reaction centre se upward blue arrow Primary acceptor tak electron donation dikhata hai; primary acceptor reaction centre ka electron sabse pehle leta hai. Kya zigzag aur blue arrow ek hi cheez ka transfer dikhate hain? Dekho, zigzag antenna mein excitation-energy transfer hai, jabki blue arrow reaction centre se acceptor ko electron transfer hai. Electron donation se donor aur acceptor par alag charges bante hain; is charge separation se light capture ek chemical event se judta hai.

Kitaab se bahar: Photon, excitation energy aur charge separation ke basic meanings electron-transfer figure samajhne ke liye add kiye.

Leader lines identify the reaction centre and several pigment molecules.

Dekho, reaction-centre chlorophyll a electron donate karta hai; peripheral chlorophyll b ki role yahaan energy collect karke centre tak pahunchane ki thi. Is ek event mein photon ki energy ka contribution aur reaction centre se nikle electron ka route alag pehchaan mein aa gaya.

samjhana11.5-k7
Dono photosystems ke special reaction centres ko ab unki characteristic absorption wavelengths se pehchaanenge. Absorption peak woh wavelength hai jahaan absorption maximum hota hai; nm ki unit hum pehle khol chuke hain. PS I ka centre 700 nm peak ke kaaran P700, aur PS II ka centre 680 nm maximum ke kaaran P680 kehlaata hai. Kya alag peak names ka matlab alag pigment types hain? Aksar log sochte hain ki P700 aur P680 alag pigment types hain, par asal mein dono special chlorophyll a centres hain. Dekho, in names ke numbers characteristic peak wavelengths batate hain, pigment molecules ki count nahi.
Screen par: Usi membrane view ke PS I aur PS II centres par labels: PS I—chlorophyll a—P700—700 nm; PS II—chlorophyll a—P680—680 nm.
Terms: absorption peak = Woh wavelength jahaan pigment ka light absorption maximum hota hai.; P700 = PS I ka chlorophyll a reaction centre, jiska characteristic absorption peak 700 nm hai.; P680 = PS II ka chlorophyll a reaction centre, jiska characteristic absorption maximum 680 nm hai.
Yaad: 11.4-k6
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p008-i12 The single chlorophyll a molecule forms the reaction centre. The reaction centre is different in both the photosystems. In PS I the reaction centre chlorophyll a has an absorption peak at 700 nm, hence is called P700, while in PS II it has absorption maxima at 680 nm, and is called P680.
ruko, socho11.5-k8
Socho, phir aage badhenge: collecting antenna bahut strong ho, lekin uska functional reaction centre missing ho, toh light collection akeli chemical electron-transfer event chala degi? Dekho, collection energy centre tak laati hai, lekin acceptor ko electron dene wali functional site ke bina collected energy required photochemical event nahi bana paayegi. Antenna aur reaction centre complementary parts hain; zyada collection missing conversion site ka replacement nahi hai.
Screen par: Intact antenna mein energy pulses missing centre ke paas rukte; comparison mein intact centre se primary acceptor ko electron transfer.
Jawab: Antenna light capture karta hai, lekin functional reaction-centre chlorophyll a photochemical electron donation shuru karta hai; collection akeli us missing function ko replace nahi karti.
Aam galti: Chlorophyll b ki abundance ko reaction-centre chlorophyll a ka replacement samajhna galat hai; collection aur photochemical conversion alag jobs hain.

p009-i1 system consisting of cytochromes (Figure 11.5). This movement of electrons is downhill, in terms of an oxidation-reduction or redox potential scale. The electrons are not used up as they pass through the electron transport chain, but are passed on to the pigments of photosystem PS I. Simultaneously, electrons in the reaction centre of PS I are also excited when they receive red light of wavelength 700 nm and are transferred to another accepter [acceptor] molecule that has a greater redox potential.
analogy11.6-k4

Dekho, trolley wahi aage pahunchti hai, lekin uski height se judi energy ghatati hai; doosri lift usse naya boost deti hai. Yahaan trolley electron hai, dono powered lifts PS II aur PS I ki light-driven excitations hain, aur descending ramps carrier transfers hain. p009-i2 These electrons then are moved downhill again, this time to a molecule of energy-rich NADP⁺. The addition of these electrons reduces NADP⁺
samjhana11.6-k6
Electron lekar agle molecule ko dene wala substance carrier kehlaata hai. In carriers ko transfer ke order mein redox-potential framework par rakhne se poore route ki characteristic Z-jaisi profile milti hai. Isliye ise Z scheme kehte hain: PS II ka light boost, chain ka downhill transfer, PS I ka doosra boost, aur NADP⁺ tak transfer. Kya Z thylakoid membrane ka actual shape batata hai? Dekho, Z electron-transfer energetics ka representation hai, membrane mein bana literal zigzag raasta nahi. NADPH + H⁺ ki notation bhi milti hai; reduced carrier NADPH hai, aur saath likha H⁺ medium ka proton dikhata hai. Carrier reduction ka chemical ledger wahi NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH rahega.
Screen par: Membrane picture ke saath progressively bana energy/redox schematic: do light-driven rises aur do descending stretches; final NADP⁺ → NADPH label.
Terms: carrier = Electron receive karke agle molecule ko transfer karne wala substance.; Z scheme = Do light-driven excitations aur carrier transfers ka characteristic Z-jaisa energetic representation.
Kitaab se bahar: NADPH + H⁺ notation ko NADPH ki balanced formation reaction se alag clarify kiya.
p009-i3 to NADPH + H⁺. This whole scheme of transfer of electrons, starting from the PS II, uphill to the acceptor, down the electron transport chain to PS I, excitation of electrons, transfer to another acceptor, and finally down hill to NADP⁺ reducing it to NADPH + H⁺ is called the Z scheme, due to its characteristic shape (Figure 11.5). This shape is formed when all the carriers are placed in a sequence on a redox potential scale.
figure11.6-k7
Figure 11.5 mein left bracket Photosystem II aur right bracket Photosystem I identify karta hai; Light ki dashed arrows dono tak aati hain. Dono LHC labels wahi light harvesting complexes hain, jo captured energy centres tak pahunchate hain. PS II se upward electron arrow pehle e⁻ acceptor tak, phir descending arrows Electron transport system se PS I tak jaate hain. PS I se doosra upward arrow uske e⁻ acceptor tak, aur final transfer NADP⁺ se NADPH formation tak follow karo. Transport system ke paas ADP + iP → ATP label kya jodta hai? Dekho, iP inorganic phosphate hai, aur yeh branch electron transport ko ATP formation se jodti hai; iska mechanism aage samjhenge. PS II ke neeche H₂O → 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] shorthand hai, aur electron arrow PS II ko supply dikhata hai. [O] ek oxygen-atom equivalent ka accounting symbol hai; released gas O₂ hoti hai, jiske liye do aise equivalents judte hain.

11.6.1 Splitting of Water · 8 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 6 min
Maqsad: Ab PS II ki replacement supply ko water splitting se jodenge, aur electrons, oxygen aur protons ka hisaab unki sahi location par rakhenge.
pehle poori jhalak11.6.1-k1
Ab PS II ki replacement supply ko water splitting se jodenge, aur electrons, oxygen aur protons ka hisaab unki sahi location par rakhenge. Humne dekha tha ki PS II se electron transport chain PS I ko replacement electrons pahunchati hai. Lekin PS II khud continuously electrons kaise supply karega? Dekho, PS II ke donated electrons ki replacement water splitting se milti hai, isliye uski supply sirf pehle se maujood electrons par nahi tikti. Yahaan teen baatein follow karenge: replacement ka route, products ka balanced hisaab, aur membrane par unki release location.

p009-i21 You would then ask, How does PS II supply electrons continuously? The
samjhana11.6.1-k2
Splitting of water woh process hai jisme water se electrons, H⁺ aur oxygen milte hain; yeh PS II se associated hai. Is process ko photolysis bhi kehte hain: photo light ko aur lysis splitting ko batata hai. Water ke electrons PS II ki electron kami poori karte hain, aur PS II transport chain ke through PS I ki kami poori karta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki water splitting PS I ko directly refill karti hai, par asal mein yahaan replacement route pehle PS II se guzarta hai. p009-i22 electrons that were moved from photosystem II must be replaced. This is achieved by electrons available due to splitting of water. The splitting of water is associated with the PS II; water is split into 2H⁺, [O] and electrons. This creates oxygen, one of the net products of photosynthesis. The electrons needed to replace those removed from photosystem I are provided by photosystem II.

example11.6.1-k4
Ab do water molecules ka batch lo: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. H⁺ aur electron ke charges hum pehle khol chuke hain; 4e⁻ yahaan chaar electrons ki count dikhata hai. Do H₂O mein total chaar hydrogen atoms aur do oxygen atoms hain. Products mein chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ molecule wahi atom-count sambhalte hain; chaar electrons electron-supply ledger mein aate hain. Charge ka balance bhi milta hai? Dekho, reactant water ka total charge zero hai, aur products ke +4 proton charges tatha −4 electron charges milkar zero dete hain. Isliye har ek O₂ ke saath do waters ki oxidation se chaar replacement electrons aur chaar H⁺ milte hain. Water electrons deta hai, isliye yeh water ki oxidation hai; light-driven machinery is electron extraction ko possible banati hai.

11.6.2 Cyclic and Non-cyclic Photo-phosphorylation · 7 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 14 min
Maqsad: Agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega, aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?

Kitab ki baatein (45): kitab kehti hai 14 · shabd ka matlab 13 · pehle ki zaroori baat 7 · figure ka nishaan 7 · teacher ki extra baat 4
f1 kitab kehti hai Jeev oxidisable substances se energy nikalte hain aur use bond energy ke roop mein rakhte hain.
f2 kitab kehti hai: ATP ATP jaise khaas molecules yeh energy apne chemical bonds mein lekar chalte hain.
f3 kitab kehti hai: phosphorylation Cell mitochondria aur chloroplast mein ATP banata hai; is kaam ko phosphorylation kehte hain.
f4 kitab kehti hai: Photo-phosphorylation Roshni mein ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banna photo-phosphorylation hai.
f5 kitab kehti hai: non-cyclic Do photosystem series mein kaam karein, pehle PS II phir PS I, toh non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation hoti hai.
f6 kitab kehti hai: electron transport chain Dono photosystem electron transport chain se jude hain, jaise Z scheme mein dekha.
f7 kitab kehti hai Is electron flow se ATP aur NADPH + H⁺ dono bante hain (Figure 11.5).
f8 kitab kehti hai: cyclic Figure 11.6: PS I ka electron acceptor aur electron transport system se hokar P 700 par laut aata hai; saath mein ADP + Pi se ATP banta hai.
f9 kitab kehti hai Jab sirf PS I kaam kare, electron photosystem ke andar ghoomta hai aur cyclic flow se phosphorylation hoti hai.
f10 kitab kehti hai: stroma lamellae Iski ek sambhav jagah stroma lamellae hai.
f11 kitab kehti hai Grana ki lamellae mein PS I aur PS II dono hain; stroma lamellae mein PS II aur NADP reductase nahi hote.
f12 kitab kehti hai Excited electron NADP⁺ ko nahi jaata; electron transport chain se PS I complex par laut aata hai.
f13 kitab kehti hai Cyclic flow se sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH + H⁺ nahi.
f14 kitab kehti hai Jab excitation ke liye sirf 680 nm se aage ki wavelength mile, tab bhi cyclic photophosphorylation hoti hai.
f15 shabd ka matlab: oxidisable substances Jo cheezein oxidation mein electron de sakein, jaise glucose.
f16 shabd ka matlab: bond energy Atomon ko jodne wale chemical bonds mein band energy.
f17 shabd ka matlab: ATP Adenosine triphosphate: tri yaani teen phosphate.
f18 shabd ka matlab: ADP Adenosine diphosphate: di yaani do phosphate.
f19 shabd ka matlab: inorganic phosphate Akela phosphate jo kisi molecule se juda nahi; chhote mein Pᵢ.
f20 shabd ka matlab: phosphorylation ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banana.
f21 shabd ka matlab: Photo-phosphorylation Photo yaani roshni: roshni ki madad se phosphorylation.
f22 shabd ka matlab: electron transport chain Carriers ki kadi jo electron ko ek se doosre tak aage deti hai.
f23 shabd ka matlab: non-cyclic Jo gol chakkar na ho: electron shuru par wapas nahi lautta.
f24 shabd ka matlab: cyclic Gol chakkar wala: electron jahan se chala, wahin laut aata hai.
f25 shabd ka matlab: NADP reductase Reductase yaani reduction karaane wala enzyme; NADP⁺ se NADPH banwata hai.
f26 shabd ka matlab: stroma lamellae Grana ko jodne wali patli, bina dher wali membrane; lamella yaani patli parat.
f27 shabd ka matlab: wavelength Roshni ki lehar mein ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori.
f28 pehle ki zaroori baat: oxidation Oxidation yaani electron dena, reduction yaani electron lena.
f29 pehle ki zaroori baat: Z scheme Z scheme: paani se PS II, phir carriers ki kadi, phir PS I, aakhir NADP⁺ (heading 11.6).
f30 pehle ki zaroori baat: grana Grana: thylakoid ki chapti thailiyon ke dher.
f31 pehle ki zaroori baat: P680 PS II ka reaction centre P680, PS I ka P700.
f32 pehle ki zaroori baat: splitting of water Paani tootne se electron, H⁺ aur O₂ milte hain (heading 11.6.1).
f33 figure ka nishaan: Photosystem I Figure ka bracket: yahan sirf PS I ka raasta dikhaya hai.
f34 figure ka nishaan: Chlorophyll P 700 PS I ka reaction centre; roshni pakadta hai aur lautta electron wapas leta hai.
f35 figure ka nishaan: Light Peeli dashed teer: roshni P 700 par padti hai.
f36 figure ka nishaan: e⁻ acceptor Uchhla electron sabse pehle pakadne wala molecule.
f37 figure ka nishaan: Electron transport system Carriers ki kadi; electron ko wapas P 700 tak le aati hai.
f38 figure ka nishaan: ADP + Pi ATP banne ki shuruaati cheezein; Pi yaani inorganic phosphate.
f39 figure ka nishaan: ATP Kadi ke paas banne wala product.
f40 teacher ki extra baat Cyclic raaste mein PS II nahi, isliye paani nahi tootta aur O₂ nahi banta.
f41 teacher ki extra baat Kabhi paudhe ko NADPH se zyada ATP chahiye; iska kaaran Calvin cycle mein aage (heading 11.7).
f42 teacher ki extra baat NADPH + H⁺ likhne ka arth: NADPH ke saath ek H⁺ bhi likha jaata hai.
f43 pehle ki zaroori baat: chloroplast Chloroplast aur mitochondria: cell ke do hisse, jahan ATP banta hai.
f44 pehle ki zaroori baat: PSI PS I ko PSI aur PS II ko PSII bhi likhte hain: do photosystem.
f45 teacher ki extra baat PS I aur PS II ke number khoj ke kram se pade: PS I pehle khoja gaya; kaam mein pehle PS II aata hai.
recap11.6.2-k1
Pichhli film mein humne dekha ki PS II ka khoya hua electron paani se aata hai. Do paani ke molecule tootte hain, aur chaar electron, chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ milte hain. Usse pehle Z scheme mein electron ka poora safar dekha tha: paani se PS II, phir carriers ki kadi, phir PS I, aur aakhir mein NADP⁺. Yaad rakhein: is safar mein electron ek hi disha mein chalta hai, paani se shuru hokar NADP⁺ par khatam. Aage hum dekhenge ki yeh safar hamesha aise hi chalta hai ya nahi. Ab socho: agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega? Aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?
Screen par: Z scheme ka raasta ek chalti line ki tarah: paani, PS II, carriers ki kadi, PS I, NADP⁺; paani ke do molecule toot kar 4 e⁻, 4 H⁺ aur ek O₂; yaad rakhein: electron ek hi disha mein, paani se NADP⁺ tak (ek teer); aakhri line par PS I ke aage NADP⁺ wala raasta band.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.2-k2
Is sawaal ka jawab isi film mein milega. Par usse pehle ek zaroori cheez pakki kar lo: ATP. Har jeev ko kaam ke liye energy chahiye: saans lene ke liye, chalne ke liye, ek naya molecule banane ke liye. Yeh energy jeev kuch cheezon se nikaalte hain, jaise khaane ka glucose. Yaad karo, oxidation ka matlab hai electron dena, aur reduction ka matlab hai electron lena. Jo cheezein oxidation mein electron de sakti hain, unhe oxidisable substances kehte hain. Glucose aisi hi ek cheez hai. Glucose se energy kaise nikalti hai, yeh aap aage respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki aisi cheezon se energy nikalti hai. Nikli hui energy jeev turant kharch nahi karte. Use molecules ke andar band karke rakh lete hain. Band karke isliye rakhte hain kyunki energy ki zaroorat har jagah aur har pal hoti hai, aur band energy ko jahan chaaho wahan le ja sakte ho. Socho ek gulel, jiski rubber ko kheench kar pakad liya. Energy us khinchi hui rubber mein band hai; chhodoge toh kaam karegi. Molecule mein atom aapas mein bandhanon se jude hote hain, jinhe chemical bonds kehte hain. In bonds mein bhi aise hi energy band rehti hai; ise bond energy kehte hain. ATP ek aisa hi khaas molecule hai. Yeh apne chemical bonds mein energy lekar chalta hai, aur cell ke har kaam tak pahunchata hai. ATP ka poora naam hai adenosine triphosphate. Tri yaani teen, toh isme teen phosphate jude hain. ADP yaani adenosine diphosphate. Di yaani do, toh isme do hi phosphate hain. ATP ko ek bhari hui battery samjho, aur ADP ko ek khaali hoti battery. Khaali battery ko phir se bharna hai, toh ADP mein ek phosphate aur jodna padta hai. Aur is jodne mein energy lagti hai. Jo phosphate akela hai, kisi molecule se juda nahi, use inorganic phosphate kehte hain. Chhote mein ise Pᵢ likhte hain; i yaani inorganic. ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banane ko phosphorylation kehte hain. Phosphate jodna, isliye naam phosphorylation. Cell yeh kaam do jagah karta hai: mitochondria mein aur chloroplast mein. Mitochondria wala tareeka bhi aap respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Hamari kahani chloroplast ki hai. Chloroplast mein yeh charging roshni se hoti hai, bilkul waise jaise solar charger dhoop se phone charge karta hai. Roshni ki madad se ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banane ko photo-phosphorylation kehte hain, kyunki photo ka matlab hi roshni hai.
Screen par: Energy ki zaroorat ke chhote drishya (saans, chalna, naya molecule); glucose se energy; oxidation = electron dena, reduction = electron lena; gulel ki khinchi rubber (apni tasveer); atomon ke beech chemical bond mein band energy; ATP: adenosine ke saath teen phosphate, ADP: do phosphate; bhari aur khaali battery (apni tasveer); ADP + Pᵢ → ATP, jodne mein energy; mitochondria aur chloroplast; dhoop se phone bharta solar charger (apni tasveer); roshni ke saath ADP + Pᵢ → ATP.
Terms: oxidisable substances = jo oxidation mein electron de sakein; bond energy = chemical bonds mein band energy; inorganic phosphate = akela phosphate, Pᵢ; phosphorylation = ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banana; Photo-phosphorylation = roshni ki madad se phosphorylation
p009-i27 Living organisms have the capability of extracting energy from oxidisable substances and store this in the form of bond energy. Special substances like ATP, carry this energy in their chemical bonds. The process through which
p010-i1 ATP is synthesised by cells (in mitochondria and chloroplasts) is named phosphorylation. Photo-phosphorylation is the synthesis of ATP from ADP and inorganic phosphate in the presence of light.
samjhana11.6.2-k3
Ab Figure 11.5 wali Z scheme yaad karo, jo hum pehle padh chuke hain. Usme do photosystem the: pehle PS II, phir PS I. Dono par roshni padti thi. Dono ke beech carriers ki ek kadi thi, jo electron ko ek se doosre tak aage deti hai. Is kadi ko electron transport chain kehte hain: electron ka transport, yaani le jaana, ek chain mein. Ise ek relay daud ki tarah socho. Har khiladi baton ko agle khiladi ke haath mein deta hai, aur baton aage badhta jaata hai. Yahan baton hai electron, aur khiladi hain carriers. Roshni do jagah zor ka dhakka deti hai: PS II par aur PS I par. Is daud mein baton paani se chalta hai. PS II aur PS I se hokar, aakhir mein NADP⁺ ke haath mein pahunchta hai. NADP⁺ electron le leta hai aur NADPH ban jaata hai. Baton wahin ruk gaya; woh wapas shuru par nahi aata. Toh is raaste mein do cheezein banti hain: ATP aur NADPH. Saath mein paani tootne se O₂ bhi nikalti hai. Aksar ise NADPH + H⁺ likhte hain. Iska matlab bas itna hai ki NADPH ke saath ek H⁺ bhi likha jaata hai. Dhyan do, electron ek seedhe raaste par aage gaya, aur jahan se chala tha wahan wapas nahi lauta. Raasta gol nahi hai. Isliye ise non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation kehte hain. Cyclic yaani gol chakkar wala, aur non-cyclic yaani jo gol chakkar na ho. Yaad rakho: non-cyclic mein dono photosystem ek ke baad ek kaam karte hain, pehle PS II, phir PS I. Aur ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai. Yahan exam ki ek baareek baat hai: naam PS I hai, par kaam mein woh doosre number par aata hai. Number kaam ke kram se nahi pade; PS I pehle khoja gaya tha, isliye uska naam I pada.
Screen par: Z scheme: PS II aur PS I, dono par roshni, beech mein carriers ki kadi; relay daud (apni tasveer): baton electron, khiladi carriers; electron paani se NADP⁺ tak pahunch kar NADPH; ATP, NADPH aur O₂; seedha raasta jo shuru par wapas nahi aata; kaam ka kram PS II pehle, PS I baad mein, aur naam ka kram khoj se (PS I pehle khoja gaya).
Terms: electron transport chain = carriers ki kadi jo electron aage deti hai; non-cyclic = jo gol chakkar na ho
Kitaab se bahar: PS I aur PS II ke naam khoj ke kram se pade, yeh kitaab mein nahi; exam ki baareek baat ke roop mein joda.
Kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki PS I pehle kaam karta hai kyunki uska number pehla hai.
p010-i2 When the two photosystems work in a series, first PS II and then the PS I, a process called non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation occurs. The two photosystems are connected through an electron transport chain, as seen earlier – in the Z scheme. Both ATP and NADPH + H⁺ are synthesised by this kind of electron flow (Figure 11.5).
figure11.6.2-k4
Ab Figure 11.6 dekho. Yahan ek bilkul alag raasta hai. Sabse upar bracket mein likha hai Photosystem I. Matlab is figure mein sirf PS I ka kaam dikhaya gaya hai; ise kahin-kahin PSI bhi likha milta hai. Neeche likha hai Chlorophyll P 700. Yeh PS I ka reaction centre hai, jo lagbhag 700 nm ki roshni pakadta hai. Peeli dashed teer par Light likha hai. Roshni seedhe P 700 par padti hai. Roshni padte hi P 700 ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai. Ungli rakho: gulabi teer seedhe upar e⁻ acceptor tak jaata hai. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai. Aur e⁻ acceptor woh molecule hai jo uchhla hua electron sabse pehle pakadta hai. Ab acceptor se ek mudi hui gulabi teer neeche Electron transport system tak aati hai. Yeh carriers ki wahi kadi hai. Ab ungli aage le chalo. Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer kahan jaati hai? Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer wapas P 700 par hi pahunchti hai, wahin jahan se electron chala tha. Electron ne ek gol chakkar poora kiya: P 700 se acceptor, phir kadi, phir wapas P 700. Isi gol chakkar ki wajah se ise cyclic kehte hain. Relay wali daud yaad hai? Yahan baton kisi ko diya hi nahi gaya. Khiladi track ka ek chakkar lagakar wahin laut aaya jahan se chala tha. Ab kadi ke paas ek kaali mudi teer dekho: ADP + Pi se ATP. Yaani is chakkar ke saath ATP banta hai. Isliye ise cyclic photophosphorylation kehte hain: roshni se, ek gol chakkar ke saath, ATP banana. Aur yeh bhi dekho ki figure mein kya nahi hai: na PS II hai, na NADP⁺ tak jaane wala raasta.
Screen par: Cyclic photophosphorylation ka figure jaisa drishya, har label tabhi jab line uska naam le: Photosystem I bracket, Chlorophyll P 700, Light ki peeli dashed teer, upar e⁻ acceptor tak gulabi teer, Electron transport system, wapas P 700 tak teer, ADP + Pi → ATP; relay mein khiladi track ka chakkar lagakar lautta (apni tasveer); aakhir mein dikhe ki PS II aur NADP⁺ ka raasta nahi hai.
Terms: cyclic = gol chakkar wala
p010-i3 Cyclic photophosphorylation: a dashed yellow arrow carries Light to Chlorophyll P 700. A straight pink arrow rises from P 700 to the e⁻ acceptor. A curved pink arrow leads from the acceptor down to the Electron transport system, and another pink arrow returns from that system to P 700. Beside the transport system, a curved black arrow indicates ADP + Pi → ATP. The Photosystem I bracket identifies the PSI pathway. No PS II or NADP⁺-reduction branch is shown.
samjhana11.6.2-k5
Toh shuruaat ka sawaal: jab NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta na ho, toh electron kahan jaata hai? Jab NADP⁺ tak raasta na ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se hokar wapas PS I par laut aata hai. Aisa tab hota hai jab sirf PS I kaam kar raha ho. Electron PS I ke andar hi chakkar lagata rehta hai. Is gol bahav ke saath jo phosphorylation hoti hai, wahi cyclic photo-phosphorylation hai. Par chloroplast mein aisa kahan ho sakta hai, yeh samajhne ke liye pehle uske andar ka naksha yaad karo. Chloroplast mein thylakoid ki chapti thailiyan sikkon ki tarah ek ke upar ek rakhi hoti hain. Inke dher grana kehlate hain. Do dheron ko jodne wali patli, bina dher wali membrane bhi hoti hai, jaise do imaraton ke beech ka galiyara. Inhe hum pehle stroma lamellae ke naam se dekh chuke hain; lamella yaani patli parat. Grana ki membrane mein dono photosystem hote hain, PS I bhi aur PS II bhi. Par stroma lamellae ki membrane mein PS II nahi hota. Aur NADP reductase enzyme bhi nahi hota. NADP reductase woh enzyme hai jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banne ka kaam karata hai. Reductase yaani reduction karaane wala. Toh socho, stroma lamellae mein uchhle electron ko NADP⁺ tak pahunchane wala enzyme hi nahi hai. Isliye electron ke paas wapas lautne ke siva koi raasta nahi. Isliye stroma lamellae ko cyclic photo-phosphorylation ki ek sambhav jagah maana jaata hai. Nateeja pakka yaad rakho: gol raaste mein sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH + H⁺ nahi banta. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge. Par aisa nahi hai: gol raaste mein electron NADP⁺ tak jaata hi nahi, aur PS II ke bina paani bhi nahi tootta, isliye sirf ATP banta hai. Ek aur haalat hai. Yaad karo, roshni lehron mein chalti hai, aur ek lehar ki chot se agli chot tak ki doori wavelength hai. PS II ka centre P680 hai, jo lagbhag 680 nm ki roshni chahta hai. PS I ka P700, 700 nm tak ki roshni bhi pakad leta hai. Jab sirf 680 nm se aage ki roshni mile, toh PS II theek se nahi jaagta. Sirf PS I chalta hai, aur cyclic photo-phosphorylation hoti hai. Aksar log ise ulta samajh lete hain, par 680 nm se aage ka matlab hai 680 se zyada, yaani lambi wavelength, kam wavelength nahi. Paudhe ko yeh gol raasta kyon chahiye, iska ek bada kaaran hum aage Calvin cycle mein dekhenge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki kabhi paudhe ko NADPH se zyada ATP chahiye hota hai.
Screen par: Electron wapas PS I par; chloroplast ka naksha: grana sikkon ke dher jaise, unhe jodti stroma lamellae galiyare jaisi; grana ki membrane mein PS I aur PS II, stroma lamellae mein sirf PS I (PS II aur NADP reductase nahi); lautta electron; sirf ATP; 680 nm aur 700 nm ki lehrein, 680 se lambi lehar.
Terms: NADP reductase = reduction karaane wala enzyme jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banwata hai
p010-i12 When only PS I is functional, the electron is circulated within the photosystem and the phosphorylation occurs due to cyclic flow of electrons (Figure 11.6). A possible location where this could be happening is in the stroma lamellae. While the membrane or lamellae of the grana have both PS I and PS II the stroma lamellae membranes lack PS II as well as NADP reductase enzyme. The excited electron does not pass on to NADP⁺ but is cycled back to the PS I complex through the electron transport chain (Figure 11.6). The cyclic flow hence, results only in the synthesis of ATP, but not of NADPH + H⁺. Cyclic photophosphorylation also occurs when only light of wavelengths beyond 680 nm are available for excitation.
example11.6.2-k6
Ab ek electron ko pakad kar stroma lamellae mein uska poora safar chalate hain. Roshni P 700 par padi, aur uska ek electron energy lekar uchhal gaya. e⁻ acceptor ne use pakda, aur woh carriers ki kadi mein aage badha. Kadi ke aage use NADP⁺ tak le jaane wala NADP reductase mila hi nahi, kyunki yahan woh enzyme hai hi nahi. Toh electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa gaya, aur agli roshni ka intezaar karne laga. Is ek chakkar ke saath ATP bana. NADPH ek bhi nahi bana, aur O₂ bhi nahi nikli.
Screen par: Stroma lamellae mein ek electron ka safar, kadam-kadam ek hi tasveer par: P 700 par roshni, electron uchhla, e⁻ acceptor, kadi, NADP reductase ki khaali jagah, wapas P 700; ATP bana, NADPH aur O₂ nahi.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
ruko, socho11.6.2-k7
Ruko, pehle khud socho: agar kisi patti par sirf 700 nm wali roshni pade, toh usme NADPH banega ya nahi? Aapne socha hoga ki NADPH nahi banega, aur yahi sahi hai. Aisi roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, aur sirf ATP banega. Ab dono raaste ek saath rakh kar dekho. Seedhe raaste, yaani non-cyclic, mein electron paani se chalta hai: pehle PS II, phir PS I, aur aakhir NADP⁺. ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno bante hain. Gol raaste, yaani cyclic, mein sirf PS I kaam karta hai, aur electron wapas P 700 par aata hai. Sirf ATP banta hai; na NADPH, na O₂. Ab shuruaat ka sawaal phir se: NADP⁺ ka raasta band ho, toh electron kahan jaayega, aur kya kuch banega? Raasta band ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa jaata hai. Aur tab bhi kuch banta hai: ATP, bas NADPH nahi. Ab ek ajeeb baat par gaur karo, jo hamari agli film ka sawaal hai: dono raaston mein ATP bana, par electron ne kahin bhi ADP ko chhua tak nahi. Toh ATP bana kaise? ATP bana kaise, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Sawaal screen par; dono raaste saath-saath: non-cyclic (paani, PS II, PS I, NADP⁺; ATP, NADPH, O₂) aur cyclic (PS I se wapas P 700; sirf ATP); shuruaat ka sawaal phir screen par, NADP⁺ ka band raasta; electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700, ATP bana, NADPH nahi; aakhir mein electron aur ADP ke beech khaali jagah, sawaal khula.
Jawab: Sirf 700 nm ki roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, sirf ATP banega, NADPH nahi.
Aam galti: Yeh sochna ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.6.3 Chemiosmotic Hypothesis · 13 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 15 min
Maqsad: Electron transport ki energy seedhe ADP ko nahi milti, toh ATP banane tak kaise pahunchti hai?
Misaal: Baandh aur turbine: paani ke levels ka fark proton gradient hai, baandh thylakoid membrane hai, aur bahav se kaam karne wali turbine ATP synthase hai.
Kitab ki baatein (70): kitab kehti hai 19 · shabd ka matlab 27 · pehle ki zaroori baat 6 · figure ka nishaan 18 · teacher ki extra baat 0
f1 pehle ki zaroori baat Thylakoid membrane ke andar lumen aur bahar stroma hai; H⁺ positive hydrogen ion hai.
f2 pehle ki zaroori baat ADP aur inorganic phosphate ko jodkar ATP banane ke liye energy chahiye.
f3 kitab kehti hai Photosynthesis aur respiration mein ATP synthesis membrane ke across proton gradient se judi hai.
f4 shabd ka matlab: chemiosmotic hypothesis H⁺ ka membrane ke dono taraf fark aur unka lautna ATP synthesis kaise chalaate hain, iska explanation.

f64 shabd ka matlab: hypothesis Samjhaane ke liye pehle rakha gaya vichaar, jise experiments se parkha jaata hai.
f65 shabd ka matlab: chemiosmotic Naam mein chemi chemical kaam se aur osmotic membrane ke paar movement se jodta hai; yahaan proton movement ATP formation se judta hai.
f66 shabd ka matlab: gradient Dhalaan jaisa fark: ek taraf zyada, doosri taraf kam; particles apne gradient ki disha mein jaa sakte hain.
f67 shabd ka matlab: electrochemical Electro electrical charge ke fark aur chemical concentration ke fark ko jodta hai.
f68 shabd ka matlab: synthase Kisi substance ko banane wala enzyme.
f69 shabd ka matlab: diffusion Particles ka apne-aap zyada concentration se kam concentration ki taraf phailna.
f70 shabd ka matlab: conformational Protein ki shape, ya conformation, se juda.
samjhana11.6.3-k1
Humne dekha ki cyclic aur non-cyclic electron flow, dono se ATP ban sakta hai. Electron transport ki energy seedhe ADP ko nahi milti, toh ATP banane tak kaise pahunchti hai? Electron transport ki energy ATP banane tak H⁺ ka fark banaakar pahunchti hai; unka lautna ATP synthesis chalaata hai. Thylakoid ki band thaili yaad karo: andar lumen hai aur bahar stroma hai. H⁺ positive hydrogen ion hai; ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banane mein energy lagti hai. Ab ek dhalaan socho: ek taraf H⁺ zyada hain, doosri taraf kam hain. Isi zyada-se-kam ki dhalaan ko gradient kehte hain; iske saath particles apne-aap kam wali taraf jaa sakte hain. Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki concentration aur electrical haalat ke fark ko proton gradient kehte hain. Is fark ko electrochemical kehte hain: electro yaani charge ka fark; chemical yaani concentration ka fark. Ab ATP banne ko samjhaane ke liye ek vichaar rakho: H⁺ jama honge, phir unka lautna ATP banwaayega. Is tarah pehle rakhi gayi explanation ko hypothesis kehte hain; use experiments se jaanchte hain, sirf maan nahi lete. Hamaari is hypothesis mein membrane paar H⁺ ka jaana chemical kaam, yaani ATP banana, chalaata hai. Isi jod ka naam chemiosmotic hai: chemi yahaan chemical kaam batata hai, aur osmotic membrane ke paar movement. Isliye ATP banne ki is explanation ko chemiosmotic hypothesis kehte hain. Photosynthesis mein H⁺ thylakoid lumen mein jama hote hain, stroma mein nahi. Respiration mein bhi membrane ke aar-paar proton gradient ATP synthesis se juda hai. Respiration ka poora kaam aage dekhenge; abhi mitochondrion ki outer aur inner membrane socho. Inke beech ki jagah intermembrane space hai; inter yahaan beech ka matlab deta hai. Respiration ki electron transport system, ya ETS, chalne par H⁺ isi space mein jama hote hain. Yaad rakho, mitochondrial accumulation intermembrane space mein hai, matrix mein nahi.
Screen par: Chloroplast cutaway mein lumen aur stroma; mitochondrial cutaway mein outer aur inner membranes ke beech H⁺. Proton concentration aur charge ke fark alag dikhte hain.
Terms: gradient = Dhalaan jaisa fark; ek taraf zyada aur doosri taraf kam.; proton gradient = Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki concentration aur electrical haalat ka fark.; electrochemical = Electro charge aur chemical concentration ke fark se juda.; chemiosmotic hypothesis = Proton gradient aur proton return se ATP synthesis samjhaane wala vichaar.; hypothesis = Pehle rakha explanation jise experiments se parkhte hain.; chemiosmotic = Chemi chemical kaam aur osmotic membrane ke paar movement se jodta hai.; intermembrane space = Mitochondrion ki outer aur inner membrane ke beech ki jagah.
Kitaab se bahar: Mitochondrial double-membrane arrangement ka zaroori base yahaan diya hai; naam ke chemi aur osmotic hisse samajhne ke liye khole hain.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k9, 11.6.1-k5, 11.6.2-k2
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p010-i14 Let us now try and understand how actually ATP is synthesised in the chloroplast. The chemiosmotic hypothesis has been put forward to explain the mechanism. Like in respiration, in photosynthesis too, ATP synthesis is linked to development of a proton gradient across a membrane. This time these are the membranes of thylakoid. There is one difference though, here the proton accumulation is towards the inside of the membrane, i.e., in the lumen. In respiration, protons accumulate in the intermembrane space of the mitochondria when electrons move through the ETS (Chapter 12).
analogy11.6.3-k2
Aap ek baandh socho: ek taraf paani jama hai, doosri taraf level neecha hai. Paani upar jama karne mein energy lagi; ab use turbine ke raaste neeche behne do. Us bahav se turbine kaam kar sakti hai; jama energy ab kaam mein aa rahi hai. Yahaan baandh thylakoid membrane hai, paani ke levels ka fark proton gradient hai. Turbine ki jagah ATP banane wala enzyme socho; iska naam ATP synthase hai. Synthase ka matlab kisi cheez ko banaane wala enzyme hai; yahaan banne wali cheez ATP hai. Ab hum pehle H⁺ jama hona, phir unke lautne se ATP banna samjhenge.
Screen par: Baandh aur turbine ke saath thylakoid membrane, proton gradient aur ATP synthase ki tulna.
Terms: synthase = Kisi substance ko banane wala enzyme.
Kitaab se bahar: Baandh aur turbine ki analogy energy jama hone aur usse kaam lene ko jodti hai.
Kya kis jaisa: Baandh membrane, water-level difference gradient aur turbine ATP synthase hai.
Kahan tootti hai: H⁺ gravity se nahi, electrochemical gradient ki disha mein behte hain.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
pehle poori jhalak11.6.3-k3
Yaad karo, light electron ko excited karti hai; carriers use aage le jaate hain. Isi excited hone ko yahaan activation of electrons kehte hain. Ab hum isi safar mein gradient banane wale teen kaam dhoondhenge. Pehla, water splitting lumen mein H⁺ deti hai. Doosra, carrier stroma se H⁺ lumen mein le jaata hai. Teesra, NADPH banne mein stroma ke H⁺ istemal hote hain. Phir dekhenge ki H⁺ ka ATP synthase se lautna kya kaam karta hai.
Screen par: Wahi thylakoid cutaway; teen numbered jagah par water splitting, carrier transport aur stromal NADP⁺ reduction.
Terms: activation of electrons = Light ki energy se electron ka excited hona.
Yaad: 11.6-k3, 11.6-k5
p010-i15 Let us understand what causes the proton gradient across the membrane. We need to consider again the processes that take place during the activation of electrons and their transport to determine the steps that cause a proton gradient to develop (Figure 11.7).
samjhana11.6.3-k4
Water-splitting complex ki jagah yaad karo: PS II se juda, lumen ki taraf khulta hai. Do waters ka hisaab tha: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. Protons or hydrogen ions, dono yahaan H⁺ ke naam hain. Aap machinery ka face dekho; kya in H⁺ ko pehle stroma se membrane paar karni padegi? In H⁺ ko stroma se membrane paar nahi karni padegi, kyunki water splitting lumen-facing side par hoti hai. Reaction inner side par hoti hai, isliye H⁺ seedhe lumen mein milte hain. Baandh wali tasveer mein pehla kaam mila: jama hone wali taraf H⁺ badh gaye.
Screen par: Lumen-facing water splitting par do H₂O se chaar H⁺ lumen mein judte hain; electron aur oxygen arrows shaant rehte hain.
Yaad: 11.6.1-k4, 11.6.1-k5, 11.6.1-k6
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p010-i16 (a) Since splitting of the water molecule takes place on the inner side of the membrane, the protons or hydrogen ions that are produced by the splitting of water accumulate within the lumen of the thylakoids.
samjhana11.6.3-k5
Reaction centre ka electron pehle primary acceptor leta hai, phir carriers use aage dete hain. Ab aisa carrier socho jo electron ke saath hydrogen ka hissa bhi le jaaye. Isi ko H carrier kehte hain; H hydrogen ka sanket hai. Outer-side primary acceptor se electron is H carrier tak pahunchta hai. Electron lete waqt carrier stroma se H⁺ bhi leta hai. Ab aap inner side dekho: electron agle carrier ko mila, H⁺ lumen mein chhoot gaya. Water splitting ne H⁺ andar diye the; yahaan stroma ke H⁺ bhi kaise ghat gaye? Yahaan stroma ke H⁺ isliye ghate kyunki carrier unhe lekar lumen mein chhod aaya. Isi stroma-se-lumen movement ke kaaran bahar H⁺ kam aur andar zyada hote hain. Electron aur H⁺ ke safar jude hain, lekin dono ka raasta ek nahi hai.
Screen par: Wahi membrane picture mein carrier stromal H⁺ leta aur lumen mein chhodta hai; electron aur proton arrows alag rang mein.
Terms: H carrier = Electron ke saath hydrogen ka hissa le jaane wala carrier.
Yaad: 11.5-k5, 11.6-k3
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p011-i20 (b) As electrons move through the photosystems, protons are transported across the membrane. This happens because the primary acceptor of electron which is located towards the outer side of the membrane transfers its electron not to an electron carrier but to an H carrier. Hence, this molecule removes a proton from the stroma while transporting an electron. When this molecule passes on its electron to the electron carrier on the inner side of the membrane, the proton is released into the inner side or the lumen side of the membrane.
samjhana11.6.3-k6
Yaad karo, PS I ke acceptor se aaye electrons aakhir NADP⁺ ko milte hain. NADP⁺ electrons leta hai; isi electron lene ko reduction kehte hain. NADP reductase yeh badlav karaata hai; woh electrons ka mool source nahi hai. Yeh enzyme stroma ki taraf kaam karta hai; isi face ko stroma side kehte hain. Aap pehle ka hisaab dekho: NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH. NADPH banne mein yeh H⁺ kahaan se aata hai? NADPH banne mein yeh H⁺ stroma se aata hai, isliye wahaan bache H⁺ aur ghatte hain. Aksar lagta hai ki teesra kaam bhi H⁺ ko lumen bhejta hai, par aisa nahi hai. Yahaan H⁺ stromal reaction mein istemal hua; isi se dono taraf ka fark badha.
Screen par: PS I se stromal NADP reductase tak electrons; NADPH formation mein stromal H⁺ istemal hota hai, lumen ki taraf proton arrow nahi.
Terms: stroma side = Thylakoid membrane ka stroma ki taraf wala face.
Yaad: 11.6-k5, 11.6.2-k7
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p011-i21 (c) The NADP reductase enzyme is located on the stroma side of the membrane. Along with electrons that come from the acceptor of electrons of PS I, protons are necessary for the reduction of NADP⁺ to NADPH+ H⁺. These protons are also removed from the stroma.
example11.6.3-k7
Ab aap do water molecules wala example teenon kaamon se jodo. Water splitting ke chaar H⁺ lumen mein mile; carrier ne aur H⁺ andar pahunchaye. Saath hi NADPH banne mein stroma ke H⁺ istemal hue. Isliye lumen mein H⁺ zyada aur stroma mein kam hote gaye. Yeh fark thylakoid membrane ke aar-paar hai, chloroplast ki baahari boundary par nahi. Ab zyada H⁺ wale ghol ko socho: uski acidity badh jaati hai. Acidity bataane wale scale ko pH kehte hain; H⁺ ki concentration badhe toh pH ghatta hai. Isliye lumen ka pH ghatta hai, aur is kami ko naapa ja sakta hai.
Screen par: Lumen mein water splitting se +4 H⁺ aur transported H⁺; stroma mein transport aur NADPH formation se H⁺ kam. Lumen pH neeche jaata hai.
Terms: pH = Acidity ka scale; H⁺ badhne par pH ghatta hai.
Kahan tootti hai: High H⁺ ka matlab high pH nahi; lumen ki acidity badhti aur pH ghatta hai.
p011-i22 Hence, within the chloroplast, protons in the stroma decrease in number, while in the lumen there is accumulation of protons. This creates a proton gradient across the thylakoid membrane as well as a measurable decrease in pH in the lumen.
ruko, socho11.6.3-k8
Ab lumen mein H⁺ jama hain, lekin ADP mein phosphate judna abhi samjhaana baaki hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: proton gradient mein hamaari itni dilchaspi kyun hai? Proton gradient mein dilchaspi isliye hai kyunki H⁺ ka sahi raaste lautna ATP synthesis ko energy deta hai. Aap baandh se turbine ki taraf bahav yaad karo; sirf paani jama karna kaafi nahi tha. Ab hum proton return ka woh protein raasta kholenge.
Screen par: High-lumen aur low-stroma H⁺; ATP synthase ki jagah lumen-to-stroma return ka adhura raasta.
Jawab: H⁺ ka ATP synthase se lautna gradient ki energy ko ATP formation se jodta hai.
Aam galti: Sirf H⁺ jama hone se ATP nahi banta; ATP synthase aur proton return bhi chahiye.
p011-i23 Why are we so interested in the proton gradient?
samjhana11.6.3-k9
Ab aap membrane mein laga proton return ka raasta dekho. ADP aur inorganic phosphate jodkar ATP banane wala enzyme ATP synthase hai. Synthase ka banaane wala matlab hum turbine ki tasveer mein khol chuke hain. Is enzyme ke do hisse hain: membrane mein dhansa CF₀ aur stroma ki taraf nikla CF₁. CF₀ proton channel hai; CF₁ membrane ki outer stromal surface par nikla head hai. Channel membrane ke aar-paar hai; aise aar-paar faile hone ko transmembrane kehte hain. Ab particles ka apne-aap zyada concentration se kam ki taraf phailna socho. Isi phailne ko diffusion kehte hain; yahaan H⁺ electrochemical gradient ki disha mein chalte hain. Protein ka raasta milne par aise paar jaane ko facilitated diffusion kehte hain. Facilitated ka matlab yahaan raasta milne se aasaan hua hai. H⁺ CF₀ se lumen se stroma lautte hain, kyunki unka electrochemical gradient isi disha mein hai. Lautne se dono taraf ka fark ghatta hai; isi ko breakdown of this gradient kehte hain. Aksar lagta hai ki protons toot gaye, par asal mein unki dono taraf ki asamaanta ghati hai. Is fark mein jama energy proton return par milti hai, isliye ATP synthesis chal sakti hai.
Screen par: Ussi thylakoid ka enlargement: membrane mein CF₀, stroma mein CF₁; lumen → CF₀ → stroma proton arrow aur ghatta gradient.
Terms: ATP synthase = Proton flow ki energy se ADP aur phosphate jodkar ATP banane wala enzyme.; CF₀ = Membrane mein dhansa proton channel.; CF₁ = ATP synthase ka stroma ki taraf nikla head.; transmembrane = Membrane ke aar-paar faila hua.; diffusion = Particles ka apne-aap zyada concentration se kam ki taraf phailna.; facilitated diffusion = Protein ke raaste apne gradient ki disha mein paar jaana.; breakdown of this gradient = Proton return se dono taraf ka fark kam hona.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k2
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p011-i24 This gradient is important because it is the breakdown of this gradient that leads to the synthesis of ATP. The gradient is broken down due to the movement of protons across the membrane to the stroma through the transmembrane
p012-i1 channel of the CF₀ of the ATP synthase. The ATP synthase enzyme consists of two parts: one called the CF₀ is embedded in the thylakoid membrane and forms a transmembrane channel that carries out facilitated diffusion of protons across the membrane. The other portion is called CF₁ and protrudes on the outer surface of the thylakoid membrane on the side that faces the stroma.
samjhana11.6.3-k10
Aapne CF₀ ka raasta aur CF₁ ka stromal head dekh liya. Par H⁺ ka bahav ADP aur phosphate jodne ka kaam kaise chalaata hai? H⁺ ka bahav yeh kaam protein ki banawat badalne ki energy dekar chalaata hai. Protein ki teen dishaon mein phaili banawat uski conformation hai. Conformational ka matlab isi banawat se juda hai; iske badalne ko conformational change kehte hain. Gradient breakdown ki energy CF₁ mein yahi badlav karaati hai. Isse enzyme ATP ke kai molecules banaata hai; kai ke liye yahaan several kaha gaya hai. In ATP molecules mein kaam ke liye energy hoti hai, isliye inhe energy-packed keh rahe hain. Aap yahaan ek H⁺ se ek ATP ka fixed anupaat mat nikaalna.
Screen par: Ussi CF₀–CF₁ enlargement mein proton return ke saath CF₁ ki banawat badalti hai; ADP + Pᵢ se kai ATP bante hain.
Terms: conformational = Protein ki banawat, ya conformation, se juda.; conformational change = Protein ki teen-dimensional banawat mein badlav.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p012-i2 The break down of the gradient provides enough energy to cause a conformational change in the CF₁ particle of the ATP synthase, which makes the enzyme synthesise several molecules of energy-packed ATP.
poori kahani dobara11.6.3-k11
Ab hum baandh aur turbine ka poora sambandh biological naam se jodte hain. Pehle proton gradient banao, phir ATP synthase se H⁺ lautaakar ATP banao; yahi chemiosmosis hai. Chemiosmosis ko chaar cheezein chahiye: membrane, proton pump, proton gradient aur ATP synthase. Energy lagaakar H⁺ membrane ke paar jama karne wali machinery proton pump hai. Thylakoid lumen wahi andar ki jagah hai, jahaan humne high H⁺ concentration dekhi. Pehle pumping mein energy lagti hai; waapas gradient ki disha mein diffusion se energy milti hai. ATP synthase channel waapasi ka raasta deta hai; enzyme mili energy se ATP formation karaata hai. Aap order jodo: gradient bana, CF₀ se H⁺ laute, CF₁ ki banawat badli, ATP bana. Electron seedhe ADP se judkar ATP nahi bana raha.
Screen par: Wahi thylakoid picture par chaar requirements; pehle inward pumping, phir CF₀ se outward return aur CF₁ par ATP formation.
Terms: chemiosmosis = Proton gradient aur ATP synthase se proton return ko ATP formation se jodna.; proton pump = Energy lagaakar H⁺ membrane ke paar jama karne wali machinery.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p012-i3 Chemiosmosis requires a membrane, a proton pump, a proton gradient and ATP synthase. Energy is used to pump protons across a membrane, to create a gradient or a high concentration of protons within the thylakoid lumen. ATP synthase has a channel that allows diffusion of protons back across the membrane; this releases enough energy to activate ATP synthase enzyme that catalyses the formation of ATP.
figure11.6.3-k12
Ab aap Figure 11.7 par ungli rakhkar poori kahani jodo. Thylakoid membrane band boundary hai; ismein photosystems, carriers aur ATP synthase lage hain. Bahar Stroma (low H⁺), ya Stroma, hai; andar Lumen (high H⁺) mein H⁺ zyada hain. Pehle electron route dekho: Light ke arrows P680, PS II aur P700, PS I tak pahunchte hain. PS ka matlab photosystem hai; P680 aur P700 unke reaction centres hain. PS II ke paas H₂O se Oxidation of water hoti hai; ½O₂ + H⁺ uske products dikhata hai. ½O₂ ek water ka proportional oxygen hisaab hai; alag aadha oxygen molecule nahi tair raha. Water ke electrons PS II ko milte hain; ab aap arrow PQ tak dekho. PQ ka naam Plastoquinone hai; electrons aur do H⁺ lekar iska reduced form PQH₂ banta hai. Agla Cytochrome B6f membrane complex hai; B6f iski pehchaan, aur proton arrow lumen ki taraf hai. PC, ya Plastocyanin, electron ko B6f se P700, PS I tak pahunchata hai. Yahaan light doosri excitation deti hai; phir stromal side par Fd electron leta hai. Fd ferredoxin hai; isse electron FNR, ya ferredoxin–NADP⁺ reductase, tak pahunchta hai. FNR enzyme NADP⁺ reduction karaata hai; NADP⁺ + H⁺ aur NADPH labels starting substances aur product jodte hain. Ab aap H⁺ arrows alag padho: carrier stroma se leta, lumen mein chhodta hai. Water oxidation bhi lumen H⁺ badhaati hai; NADPH formation stromal H⁺ ghataati hai. Repeated H⁺ labels protons hain; green return arrow unhe ATP synthase se stroma laata hai. High Electrochemical Potential Gradient se Low ki wedge lumen-to-stroma disha dikhati hai. Is electrochemical fark mein charge aur concentration, dono ke asar yaad rakho. Return arrow membrane ke CF₀ channel se guzarta hai; CF₁, ATP synthase ka head, stroma mein hai. Head ke paas ADP + Pᵢ → ATP hai; ADP adenosine diphosphate, Pᵢ inorganic phosphate hai. ATP adenosine triphosphate hai; aap electron route NADPH tak aur proton return stroma tak alag rakho.
Screen par: Pehle bana wahi thylakoid cutaway ab Figure 11.7 ke labels ke saath; electron route, proton additions aur CF₀ return alag-alag ubharte hain.
Terms: PQ / Plastoquinone = Membrane carrier jo electrons aur stromal H⁺ leta hai.; PQH₂ = Plastoquinone ka electrons aur do H⁺ liya hua reduced form.; Cytochrome B6f = PQ aur PC ke beech electron transfer aur proton movement se juda membrane complex.; PC / Plastocyanin = B6f se PS I tak electrons pahunchane wala carrier.; Fd / Ferredoxin = PS I se FNR tak electrons pahunchane wala carrier.; FNR = Ferredoxin–NADP⁺ reductase; NADP⁺ reduction karaane wala enzyme.
Kitaab se bahar: Fd aur FNR ke full names aur PQH₂ ka reduced form hona khola gaya hai.
Yaad: 11.5-k7, 11.6-k5, 11.6.2-k2
Kahan tootti hai: ½O₂ proportional notation hai, released aadha molecule nahi.
